!
! Title: AdBlock DNS Lite
! Description: 适用于 AdGuard、AdGuardHome 的去广告合并规则，每日自动更新一次。规则源：AdGuard DNS filter、AWAvenue Ads Rule (Only.Ads)、HaGeZi DNS-Blocklists、StevenBlack hosts、anti-AD (AdGuard DNS rules)、1Hosts (Lite)。Lite 版按 .cn 域名及国内直连域名规则进行保守分类；AdGuard DNS 规则按标准语法匹配该域名及其子域名。
! Expires: 1 day
! Homepage: https://github.com/LucentDNS/adblockfilters
! Source: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/LucentDNS/adblockfilters/dist/adblockdnslite.txt
! Version: 20261127212833-f0cd9ee
! Last modified: 2026/11/27 21:28:33
! Blocked domains: 14965
! Allowed domains: 306
! Pattern block rules: 39
! Pattern allow rules: 1
!
